{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 -5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 -2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mathsf{DT}(t/2-1)\text{ for every leaf }\lambda\text{ of T}

(199)

such that,

\mathcal{A}\land\mathcal{B}\implies F\lceil_{\rho}\in\mathsf{bPTC}[k;d-1;s_{2},\ldots,s_{d}]\circ\mathsf{DT}(l)\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -7.87 L 0 7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 -5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 -2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mathsf{DT}(t-1).

(200)

Now, the probability that neither of the events occurs will be considered. For the first event, we obtain the value this event to be

\Pr[\neg\mathcal{A}]=\Pr[T\lceil_{p}\text{ has depth }\geq t/2]\leq(4ep)^{t/2}.

(201)

For the second, we sort to
Lemma

1
to translate
\mathsf{bPTC}[k;d;s_{1},s_{2},\ldots,s_{d}]\circ\mathsf{DT}(w)
to
\mathsf{bPTC}[k;d;s_{1},s_{2},\ldots,s_{d}]\circ\and_{w}
, and with
Lemma

28
we obtain

\displaystyle\Pr[\neg\mathcal{B}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\lambda}\Pr[C_{\lambda}\lceil_{p}\notin\mathsf{bPTC}[k;d-1;s_{2},\ldots,s_{d}]\circ\mathsf{DT}(l)\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -7.87 L 0 7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 -5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 -2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mathsf{DT}(t/2-1)].

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\lambda}s_{1}\cdot 2^{k}(80wp)^{t/2}

(202)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{t-1}\big(s_{1}\cdot 2^{k}(80wp)^{t/2}\big).

(203)

Combining both, we obtain,

\displaystyle\Pr[F|_{R_{p}}\notin\mathsf{bPTC}[k;d;s_{2},\ldots,s_{d}]\circ\mathsf{DT}(l)\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -7.87 L 0 7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 -5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 -2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mathsf{DT}(t-1)]

\displaystyle\leq\Pr[\neg\mathcal{A}]+\Pr[\neg\mathcal{B}]

\displaystyle\leq(4ep)^{t/2}+2^{t-1}\big(s_{1}\cdot 2^{k}(80wp)^{t/2}\big)

(204)

\displaystyle\leq(4ep)^{t/2}+\big(s_{1}\cdot 2^{k-1}(400wp)^{t/2}\big)

(205)

\displaystyle\leq s_{1}\cdot 2^{k}(400wp)^{t/2}.

(206)

∎

The previous lemma allows one to reduce the depth of a
\mathsf{bPTC}^{0}(k)
circuit. Now, this lemma can be employed sequentially to reduce the entire circuit.

.

\displaystyle+\bigg(\sum_{i=2}^{d-1}s_{i}\cdot 2^{k}\mathcal{O}(p_{i}l_{i})^{t/2}\bigg)+2^{k}m^{1/q}\mathcal{O}(p_{d}\cdot l_{d})^{t}.

as event

, and subsequent depth reductions equally for

. Building on this and based on the same

, we obtain that,

\displaystyle\leq s_{i}\cdot 2^{k}(400l_{i}p_{i})^{t/2}

\displaystyle\leq s_{i}\cdot 2^{k}\mathcal{O}(p_{i}\cdot l_{i})^{t/2}.

(208)

Now after applying

times

we obtain that

with a probability equal to the probability with the following sequence of event

, we apply

, such that for each leave, we have that,

\displaystyle\Pr[G_{\lambda|_{\rho_{d}}}\notin\mathsf{DT}(q-1)^{m}\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

\displaystyle\leq 2^{k}m^{1/q}\mathcal{O}(p_{d}\cdot l_{d})^{t}.

(211)

. However, this value will be upper bounded by the same value previously defined. Thus, we obtain that,

\displaystyle\Pr[\neg E_{1}\land\neg E_{2}\land\ldots\land E_{d}\big]=\sum_{i=1}^{d}Pr[\neg E_{i}|E_{1}\land\ldots\land E_{i-1}]

\displaystyle\leq s_{1}\cdot 2^{k}\mathcal{O}(p_{1})^{t/2}+\bigg(\sum_{i=2}^{d-1}s_{i}*2^{k}\mathcal{O}(p_{i}\cdot l_{i})^{t/2}\bigg)+2^{k}m^{1/q}\mathcal{O}(p_{d}\cdot l_{d})^{t}.

∎

In the final step, we provide a value for the probabilities of the various random restrictions and dimensions for the local decision trees to derive a new lemma. This describes based on the previous values, and the size of the global decision tree
t
, the size of the initial circuit

, and the type of circuit parametrized by

the probability of the success of reducing the circuit to decision tree of the type
\mathsf{DT}(q-1)^{m}\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

.

Lemma 34
.

Let
F\mathrel{\mathop{\mathchar 58\relax}}\{0,1\}^{n}\mapsto\{0,1\}^{m}
be an
\mathsf{bPTC}^{0}(k)
circuit of size
s
, depth
d
. Let
p=1/(s_{d}^{1/q}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\log(s)^{d-1}\cdot k^{d-1}))
. Then,

\Pr_{\rho\sim R_{p}}[F\lceil_{\rho}\notin\mathsf{DT}(q-1)^{m}\mathrel{\hbox to9.11pt{\vbox to12.08pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 8.75609pt\lower-6.04045pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.7pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -7.87 L 0 7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -10.23 -5.91}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}
{{}{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L -11.63 -2.05}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

(214)

Proof.

This follows by applying
Lemma

33
with
p_{1}=1/\mathcal{O}(1)
and
p_{2}=\ldots=p_{d-1}=1/\mathcal{O}(\log(s)+k)
and
p_{d}=1/\mathcal{O}(s_{d}^{1/q}\cdot(\log(s)+k))
.
∎
